## Quick Reference: Disconfirmed Claims

| Claim | Reality |
|-------|---------|
| "America will never shut out Chinese students" | Trump admin aggressively revoking visas (May 2025) |
| "US-China rapprochement" | SCOTUS struck down IEEPA tariffs Feb 20; Trump imposed 10% global tariff under Section 122; China tariffs at 47%; truce extended but tensions remain; Trump-Xi summit May 14-15 |
| "Germany-Russia rapprochement" | Relations frozen; economic decoupling; policy explicitly rejects rapprochement |
| "Russia as nuclear guarantor preventing strikes on Iran" | Russia did NOT prevent strikes; treaty lacks mutual defense clause |
| "Coalition includes Saudi Arabia" | Saudi refused airspace, condemned strikes on Iran |
| Trump picks Nikki Haley as VP | Trump chose JD Vance |

## Key Contextual Facts for Analysis

### US-Iran War
- **CEASEFIRE DAY 51 (May 28) — BLOCKADE DAY 46 — PROJECT FREEDOM PAUSED — Day 91; MAY 27-28 DATA CYCLE — DEAL-CLOSE TRACK GAINS DECISIVE MOMENTUM WITHOUT YET CLOSING; OIL CRASHES >5% ON RUBIO 'EVERY CHANCE' / TRUMP 'NEGOTIATING ON FUMES'; GROUND RUNG UNCHANGED: (i) President Trump held a Cabinet meeting at the White House Wednesday May 27 and declared Iran was 'negotiating on fumes,' insisting the November 2026 midterms would not factor in shaping his approach ('I don't care about the midterms'); said Iran wanted to wait him out, counting on political pressure from the midterm elections, but 'the American people understand Iran can't have a nuclear weapon'; reiterated that any deal requires Iran to give up its highly enriched uranium stockpile in exchange for sanctions relief; expressed confidence a deal is near (PBS NewsHour; NPR; CBS News; The Washington Post 'Trump gathers Cabinet as he looks to seal deal to end war that some backers worry will embolden Iran'; CNN live updates). (ii) Secretary of State Marco Rubio said talks with Iran have made some progress and the U.S. would give diplomacy 'every chance to succeed'; said President Trump has 'other options' if talks don't work — an apparent reference to renewed military strikes (CNBC; CBS News). (iii) Oil crashed on the diplomatic-progress framing: West Texas Intermediate (WTI) futures tumbled more than 5% to close at ~$88.68/bbl; Brent international benchmark lost more than 5% to settle at ~$94.29/bbl — squarely two-state deal-close pricing ($90s-low $100s), well below the sustained $130+ a ground commitment would require (CNBC 'Oil prices fall more than 5% after Rubio says U.S. will give Iran talks "every chance to succeed"'). (iv) Iran's FM spox Esmaeil Baghaei restated 'to say this means an agreement is about to be signed — no one can make such a claim'; FM Araghchi said he was unsure whether a deal was imminent; Khamenei adviser Ali Shamkhani called Trump's desired control over Iran's nuclear program 'fantasy' (NBC News; Wikipedia '2025-2026 Iran-United States negotiations'). (v) Lebanon coverage publicly named as MOU sticking point — Iran insists any ceasefire agreement negotiated with the United States must also cover Israel's operations against Hezbollah; Axios reporting on the emerging 60-day MOU specifies the Lebanon war must end ('if Hezbollah behaves, Israel will behave'); Netanyahu has expressed concerns (CBS News; Axios; CNN). (vi) Pro-Israel hawk criticism persists — Cruz/Wicker/Pompeo continue to attack the emerging 60-day MOU as 'a disaster' / 'disastrous mistake' / 'not remotely America First.' (vii) The IRGC said (May 26, amplified May 27) its air defense units intercepted and shot down a US MQ-9 Reaper drone over the Persian Gulf, also firing at an RQ-4 Global Hawk and an F-35, 'forcing them to flee' Iranian airspace; the US military has NOT commented on the IRGC's claims (PressTV; Iran International; Tasnim). Significance: signals continued low-grade kinetic skirmishing inside the maritime/air register without escalating to ground. (viii) Ukraine's General Staff: 296 combat clashes May 27 with most Russian assaults on the Pokrovsk front, where Ukrainian forces repelled ~53 attacks; Russian MLRS shelling on Kherson Korabelnyi district (including a children's playground); 8 injured in Odesa after a drone struck a shopping mall; Zelensky announced new long-range operations; second direct round still expected ~June 2 (Ukrainska Pravda; Ukrinform). (ix) Lebanon Health Ministry total since the war began holds at ≥3,185 killed / 9,633 wounded under the 45-day Israel-Lebanon ceasefire extension (May 15 start); isolated strikes continue. (x) USS Boxer ARG STILL under INDOPACOM; USS Gerald R. Ford home at Norfolk; USS Abraham Lincoln + USS George H.W. Bush CSGs + USS Tripoli ARG in the Arabian Sea; CENTCOM still reports MORE THAN 15,000 US troops committed to the Apr 13 naval blockade within the ~50,000+ CENTCOM baseline (TWZ; USNI Fleet & Marine Tracker; Atlantic Council). (xi) Venezuela: Maduro + Flores next NYC court date June 30; Cuba: blackouts persist; DPRK: no new BM test, Choe Hyon commissioning mid-June; US-China: Section 301 tariff-restoration framework remains under negotiation. PATTERN: the May 27-28 cycle is the cleanest deal-close signal since the May 24 'largely negotiated' announcement — oil down >5%, Trump openly disclaiming the midterm off-ramp, Rubio 'every chance to succeed,' Mojtaba Khamenei (per US officials) endorsing 'the broad template of the deal.' Counterweights persist (Baghaei 'no one can make such a claim,' Shamkhani 'fantasy,' IRGC unconfirmed MQ-9 claim, Lebanon-coverage and HEU-disposition sticking points, pro-Israel hawk criticism in WaPo), but the threatened operation register stays AIR/STRIKE-PACKAGE ('other options'), NOT a ground order: no new troop movements; USS Boxer ARG still INDOPACOM; USS Gerald R. Ford home; no third ARG; no BCT-scale Guard activation; no Selective Service/draft language; Murkowski draft AUMF would BAN ground troops. Ground-invasion tracker probability EASED-AT-GROUND-RUNG; status held 'no_ground_troops.' The Trump-publicly-disclaims-midterm-pressure / Rubio-'every chance' / oil-down-5%+ trio compresses war-resumption risk premium toward zero while leaving the MOU unsigned — keeping the open question live without resolving it (open-questions: nyt-intense-preparations-iran-resumption-may-2026). KEY MONITOR (next 3-7 days): (a) whether the 60-day MOU is signed within hours/days or stalls over HEU + Lebanon + frozen-asset conditions; (b) whether the IRGC executes further low-grade kinetic action and the US response stays air-only; (c) USS Boxer ARG formal CENTCOM arrival; (d) any third ARG / flank-speed expedite / BCT-scale Guard activation / Selective Service language; (e) oil trajectory (sustained $130+ = ground-commitment pricing; current Brent ~$94 / WTI ~$89 signal the opposite); (f) Russia-Ukraine ~June 2 second-round outcome.**

(iii) Indian-flagged MSV HAJI ALI (livestock vessel, ~4,000 sheep/goats Berbera→Sharjah) sank off Oman near Limah at ~03:30 May 13 after explosion + fire; all 14 Indian crew rescued; suspected drone/missile strike (Times of Israel, The National, Maritime Executive, Xinhua, Wire, Express Tribune); India MEA + UAE condemn 'unacceptable.' (iv) NBC News May 13: if ceasefire collapses, Pentagon considering renaming Operation Epic Fury → 'OPERATION SLEDGEHAMMER' to reset 60-day War Powers clock; military commanders 'have already finalized contingency plans' targeting Iranian infrastructure. (v) Senate War Powers Vote May 13: 49-50 closest yet (10th attempt); MURKOWSKI VOTED FOR FIRST TIME (joined Collins + Paul + most Dems); post-vote framing: deployed troops + warships made it difficult to accept hostilities had truly ended; Murkowski-led GOP working on AUMF that would BAN ground troops + set time limit (WLT Report, Time, Murkowski.senate.gov). (vi) Netanyahu PM's office reveals 'secret' late-March UAE visit to MBZ; UAE Ministry of Foreign Affairs DENIES; Iran FM Araghchi: 'collusion with Israel will not be forgiven' (NPR, NBC News, CBS, Canary, Middle East Eye, The Week, Hasht-e Subh, Wire). (vii) IRGC Mohammad Rasulullah Corps of Greater Tehran 5-day 'Martyred Commander Imam Khamenei' / 'Labbaik Ya Khamenei' drill — Brig Gen Hassan Hassanzadeh: IRGC + Basij commandos 'full operational readiness' (PressTV, ParsToday, News.az, Critical Threats, Euronews). (viii) Kuwait Bubiyan Island IRGC infiltration (May 1 incident) revealed May 12-14: 4 IRGC operatives captured; OIC + Kuwait MFA condemn; Iran denies as 'absolutely baseless' navigation-system malfunction; Iran 'reserves right to respond' (Al Jazeera, Middle East Monitor, The National, JPost, Aawsat, Wikipedia 2026 Bubiyan Island raid, Times Kuwait). (ix) Russia-Ukraine first direct Istanbul talks in 3 years May 15-16 (Umerov-Medinsky): produced 1,000-for-1,000 POW exchange but NO ceasefire; talks <2 hours; Moscow demands Kyiv withdraw from 4 oblasts; Kyiv demands 'full and unconditional ceasefire' 30 days; Medinsky 'satisfied'; Trump called for direct Putin meeting (Kyiv Independent, RFE/RL, The Hill, Newsweek, PBS News, Meduza). (x) Cuba grid collapse May 14: Antonio Guiteras (Cuba's largest power plant) shut down 4:58am (boiler leak); SEN available 636 MW vs demand 2,420 MW (74% unmet); 24-hour blackout in Havana; all eastern provinces stripped of power; Energy Min Vicente de la O: 'We have absolutely no fuel (oil) and absolutely no diesel'; Universal tanker ETA pushed to May 15 (Cuba Headlines, Al Jazeera, NPR, FMT, CiberCuba, Jamaica Observer). (xi) Venezuela Rodriguez at The Hague rejects Trump '51st state' framing: 'we love our independence process' (CNN, WSLS, Washington Post). (xii) Lebanon-Israel third round of direct talks in Washington May 14-15 (Karam + Hamadeh Moawad vs Dermer + Leiter); Hezbollah Qassem demands Lebanon withdraw (Washington Times, The National, US News, Haaretz). (xiii) CENTCOM May 14 X-post: 67 vessels REDIRECTED + 4 DISABLED + 15 humanitarian-aid passed. (xiv) Hapag-Lloyd CEO: $50-60M/week extra container-shipping costs from Hormuz blockage. (xv) Iran rial 1.81M/USD; inflation >73%, food 105%, bread/cereals +140%, oils/fats +219%; IMF -6.1% GDP + 68.9% inflation 2026. (xvi) BRENT $105.87/bbl May 14 (+0.22% day per Fortune); past month +11.52%; YoY +64.06%. CRITICAL CAVEATS THAT KEEP GROUND PROBABILITY LOW: (i) presentation of options is NOT execution; Trump has not ordered kinetic action; (ii) USS Ford 3-to-2-carrier reduction inconsistent with imminent ground operations; (iii) USS Boxer ARG still in Indo-Pacific transit, no flank-speed expedite; (iv) 192nd MP Battalion is logistics-support only, NOT combat-arms BCT-scale; (v) Putin Apr 29 'extremely dire consequences' warning provides Russian deterrent overlay; (vi) Trump's Germany troop-reduction threat fragments European base posture; (vii) NO third ARG, NO LHA/LHD deck-space surge, NO prepositioned sealift, NO 82nd Airborne expansion, NO BCT-scale Guard activations, NO Selective Service language. Cooper/Caine briefing crosses OPTIONS-AT-THE-BRIEFING-TABLE threshold but stops short of EXECUTION. PATTERN MOVES from CLEAR-NO-MOBILIZATION to LOW-LEVEL-MOBILIZATION-WITH-OPTIONS-ON-TABLE — first material upward shift since war began. Pivotal next windows: (a) Trump's response to Cooper/Caine briefing May 1-7; (b) Iran's revised proposal arrival Friday May 1; (c) potential in-person US-Iran meeting next week; (d) Murkowski AUMF introduction week of May 11; (e) USS Boxer ARG arrival in CENTCOM (likely first half of May).**
- CEASEFIRE DAY 23 (Apr 30) — INDEFINITE EXTENSION DAY 9 — BLOCKADE DAY 18 — Day 63; NO ground troops deployed IN Iran; GROUND OPS PROBABILITY HOLDS AT NEAR-ZERO. APR 29 DATA CYCLE BRINGS DENSEST OPERATIONAL CONFIRMATION OF BLOCKADE-AS-PRIMARY-INSTRUMENT POSTURE: (1) TRUMP REJECTS IRAN HORMUZ-FOR-BLOCKADE OFFER VIA AXIOS APR 29: 'The blockade is somewhat more effective than the bombing. They are choking like a stuffed pig. And it is going to be worse for them. They can't have a nuclear weapon.' Trump separately to reporters: Iran has to 'cry uncle' and say 'we give up.' MOST EXPLICIT US PRESIDENTIAL DOWNGRADING OF KINETIC OPTIONS OF THE WAR — by framing blockade as MORE effective than bombing, Trump has structurally rejected escalation up the kinetic ladder; rhetorical floor for ground operations LOWERED, not raised. (2) BRENT CRUDE FUTURES JUMP ~6% APR 29 TO CLOSE $118.03 (intraday $120.30, highest since June 2022); WTI $106.88 (+~7%); US gas $4.22/gal. (3) IRAN'S RIAL HITS RECORD LOW 1.8M PER USD APR 29 (-12% in one week); basic goods prices surging — confirms blockade producing intended economic pain on Iran but Iran's response remains DIPLOMATIC + RHETORICAL, NOT KINETIC. (4) REUTERS APR 29 / MANILA TIMES APR 30: IRGC effectively rules Iran, IRGC commander Ahmad Vahidi key figure; Mojtaba Khamenei sidelined. (7) CENTCOM APR 29: 29 vessels turned back since Apr 13 — REVISED LOWER from 39 Apr 28; CENTCOM refuted past reporting some ships had passed; M/V Hero II + M/V Hedy docked Chabahar after being directed back. Donetsk Q1 2026: Russia losing 316 soldiers per 1 km² gained (DOUBLE the 160 from 2025). - CEASEFIRE DAY 21 (Apr 28) — INDEFINITE EXTENSION DAY 7 — BLOCKADE DAY 16 — Day 61; NO ground troops deployed IN Iran; GROUND OPS PROBABILITY HOLDS AT NEAR-ZERO. APR 27 DATA CYCLE IS THE MOST DIPLOMATICALLY CONCRETE OF THE BLOCKADE PHASE BUT DOES NOT MOVE THE GROUND DIAL: (1) IRAN'S 'HORMUZ-FOR-BLOCKADE' PROPOSAL via Pakistani/Egyptian/Turkish/Qatari mediators — reopen Strait if US lifts naval blockade + ends war; nuclear talks postponed to later stage; sanctions lift + war-damage compensation 'of particular importance.' Trump + national security team discussed at White House Monday; multiple outlets report Trump unlikely to accept. (2) RUBIO REJECTS ON FOX NEWS: 'What they mean by opening the straits is, yes, the straits are open, as long as you coordinate with Iran, get our permission, or we'll blow you up and you pay us.' US 'cannot normalize a system in which the Iranians decide who gets to use an international waterway.' First cabinet-level public US response to Iran's offer. 'Russia will do what it can to support the interests of Iran.' 'Do everything possible to bring peace to the Middle East.' Diplomatic + uranium-custody support, NOT military supply. Lebanese President Aoun condemns Hezbollah attacks during truce. (6) HEZBOLLAH NAIM QASSEM SPEECH APR 27: direct Israel-Lebanon negotiations 'humiliating and unnecessary concession,' 'grave sin'; Hezbollah will not give up weapons; 'We will not return to pre-March status quo.' Rhetorical hardening only — no specific operational escalation step. No new US tanker seizure Apr 26-27 — scoreboard remains 4-2 US-Iran. (8) BRENT $107+ Apr 27 close (+~2-3%); WTI ~$98+. (9) WAR POWERS 60-DAY DEADLINE APR 29 (TOMORROW) / STATUTORY MAY 1 (3 DAYS) — Senate GOP blocked 5th Democratic war-powers resolution; Murkowski reportedly working on AUMF in background but no scheduled vote. Atlantic; ~3,175km from Havana — unloading uncertainty. Power deficit >1,400 MW. (12) NO US TROOP MOVEMENTS Apr 27-28. Pattern: BARGAINING-WINDOW-NEGOTIABLE IMPASSE, not escalation pivot.

Iran President Pezeshkian Apr 25 state TV: appeals to Iranians to conserve electricity ('Instead of 10 lights, two lights should be turned on') — Tehran framing war as economic-resilience attrition, NOT mobilization moment. PM Netanyahu Apr 25 orders IDF strikes 'with force' after Hezbollah 2 rockets + drones at northern Israel; 6 killed in Israeli strikes on Yater/Kafra/Houla; Hezbollah 2nd ceasefire violation; 15+ Lebanese killed since Apr 16. Troop posture Apr 26 IDENTICAL to troop posture Apr 24 — only the diplomatic schedule changed. - TRUMP CANCELS WITKOFF + KUSHNER PAKISTAN TRIP — 'WE'LL DEAL BY TELEPHONE' (Apr 25): Trump cancelled the planned Witkoff/Kushner Saturday Pakistan trip just hours before scheduled departure. Trump preserves phone-channel diplomacy: 'they can call us anytime they want.' Araghchi LEFT Islamabad BEFORE Witkoff/Kushner had even arrived — chronology means Iran's posture is 'we delivered our demands; you respond,' not 'we walked out' (Iran International, Press TV, WANA, Times of Israel, Tribune, ANI, Pravda USA, Muscat Daily)
- PEZESHKIAN ELECTRICITY-CONSERVATION APPEAL — IRAN FRAMES WAR AS ATTRITION (Apr 25): Iranian President on state TV asked Iranians to conserve electricity ('Instead of 10 lights, two lights should be turned on'); accused US/Israel of aiming to turn 'current satisfaction into dissatisfaction.' First public official acknowledgment of post-blockade strain on Iranian energy. IDF struck rocket launchers in Yater + Kafra; artillery on Houla. Lebanese Health Ministry: 6 killed in Israeli strikes Saturday. Sens Murkowski (AK), Tillis (NC), Collins (ME), Curtis (UT) want vote. SML Thune + SFRC chair Risch decline to schedule. House previously rejected withdrawal effort. Conservative read: complete absence of AUMF debate three days from 60-day mark structurally confirms ground operations not on operational horizon (Foreign Policy, CNN, The Hill, Time, ABC News, PBS NewsHour, Responsible Statecraft)

SEALED UP TIGHT' RHETORIC (Apr 23-24): Trump Truth Social ordered US Navy 'to shoot and kill any boat' laying mines in Hormuz and tripled minesweeping effort ('There is to be no hesitation'). Strike-projection surge, NOT ground-projection (no third ARG) (DNYUZ/Korea Times, Middle East Eye, Antiwar, Times of Israel, CENTCOM)

Iran chose narrowest available option: maritime mirror, not cross-domain (UKMTO, Reuters, Lloyd's List, Maritime Executive, Tasnim, Iran International, Press TV, Al Jazeera)

Apr 18-22 Yellow Line demolitions + Apr 22 Fadlallah vow operationalized. Iran's primary public precondition for talks no longer nominally intact. Critically: none of Iran's senior voices claimed Hezbollah action as Iranian retaliation — Tehran deliberately decoupling from Lebanon (Times of Israel, Al-Akhbar, Haaretz, Reuters, Al Jazeera)

- SECOND SHIP INTERDICTED — M/T TIFANI IN BAY OF BENGAL (Apr 21): Pentagon confirmed US forces boarded stateless sanctioned tanker M/T Tifani 'without incident' via helicopter fast-rope. Pentagon video shows service members rappelling onto deck. Tanker carrying Iranian oil bound for China. Geographic expansion ~3,000nm east of Hormuz. SECOND observed US boarding in 3 days, both via helicopter fast-rope = VBSS doctrinal pattern confirmed, NOT amphibious assault (Stars and Stripes, Washington Times, Fox News, US News, PBS, Military.com, Manila Times, Al Jazeera)
- VANCE ISLAMABAD TRIP CALLED OFF — WITKOFF + KUSHNER ALSO STAYED (Apr 22): VP remained at White House Tuesday; Witkoff + Kushner also did not depart. US delegation now 'TBD.' Notable: cancellation NOT substituted with military delegation (Defense Secretary, CENTCOM commander, joint chiefs all absent). Pre-invasion pattern would predict diplomatic-to-military substitution; observed pattern is full diplomatic suspension (Fox News, ABC, NPR, Axios)
- IRAN NOTIFIES PAKISTAN: NO WEDNESDAY DELEGATION — ARAGHCHI 'ACT OF WAR' (Apr 22): Tasnim reports Iran informed Pakistani mediators it would not send a delegation for Wednesday talks. FM Araghchi: blockade constitutes 'an act of war' and ceasefire violation; Iran won't negotiate 'under the shadow of threats.' Iranian rhetoric hardens but stays in MARITIME-DOMAIN register — 'act of war' deployed against blockade specifically, not reframed as ground-mobilization call (Tasnim, Al Jazeera, NBC, CNBC, Euronews)
- IRAN CLAIMS DRONE ATTACKS ON US WARSHIPS — UNCONFIRMED (Apr 21-22): Tasnim and Iran International reported Iranian drones struck US vessels in retaliation for Touska seizure — no damage reported, no US confirmation. Misbar factcheck noted circulated video appeared outdated. IF verified, first kinetic Iranian retaliation — still MARITIME-DOMAIN (US warships). Doctrinal pattern holds either way: Iran retaliation channel naval-tit-for-tat (WION, Iran International, Business Today, Tasnim, Misbar)
- KHATAM AL-ANBIYA: 'READY TO GIVE DECISIVE RESPONSE' (Apr 21): Iran commander: 'We are ready to give a decisive response to the enemy's breach of covenant.' Rhetorical reiteration of Apr 19 retaliation vow; Apr 20 family-hostage deferral framing preserved (ANI News)
- CENTCOM RELEASES TOUSKA BOARDING VIDEO (Apr 20): 33-second clip shows Marines fast-roping from MH-60S Seahawk helicopters launched from USS Tripoli (LHA-7) onto Touska's deck — confirms VBSS (Visit, Board, Search, and Seizure) doctrine. With Tifani boarding Apr 21, a pattern-of-two is established: blockade enforcement, NOT amphibious-assault doctrine (CENTCOM, USNI News, The Hill, Reuters)
- IRAN KHATAM AL-ANBIYA DEFERRED RETALIATION CITING CREW FAMILIES (Apr 20): 'delayed due to the presence of family members of the ship's crew on board, to protect their lives.' Face-saving deferral preserves rhetorical commitment without immediate kinetic action. Stated retaliation pathway remains MARITIME (Tasnim, Tabnak, Reuters, Iran International, Al Jazeera)
- TRUMP THREATENED 'KNOCK OUT EVERY POWER PLANT AND BRIDGE' (Apr 20-21, now DEFERRED via indefinite extension): 'we'll have to knock out every single Power Plant, and every single Bridge, in Iran' if no deal. FIRST EXPLICIT NAMING of civilian-dual-use infrastructure as US targets. Reescalation language was AIR-CAMPAIGN ('knock out' = bombing) — canonical air-campaign target list (Op Allied Force, Op Desert Storm), NOT ground-invasion target list. Threat paused by Apr 22 indefinite extension (CBS News, Reuters, Bloomberg, ABC News)
- 4 US GOV'T AIRCRAFT AT NUR KHAN AIR BASE (Apr 19-20): Flight tracking: 4 US aircraft (comms equipment + motorcade support) landed Sunday at Nur Khan in Rawalpindi. Pre-positioning for rescheduled Witkoff/Kushner delegation; Apr 22 trip cancellation leaves them as standby for rescheduled mission. Cargo composition is diplomatic-mission signature, not force-projection signature (The Hindu, Geo News, OSINT)
- USS SPRUANCE SEIZES IRANIAN TOUSKA (Apr 19): DDG-111 fired multiple 5-inch MK 45 rounds into engine room after 6 hours of warnings. Marines boarded and took custody. Touska transiting at 17 knots en route to Bandar Abbas. Under US Treasury sanctions. FIRST BOARDING AND FIRST VESSEL FIRED UPON of blockade (CENTCOM, CNBC, CNN, NPR, Axios, The National)
- IRAN REJECTS APR 21 TALKS VIA IRNA (Apr 19): Official IRNA: Iranian officials not participating in Islamabad talks, citing 'Washington's excessive demands, unrealistic expectations, constant shifts in stance, repeated contradictions and the ongoing naval blockade.' Iran softened hours later — 'talks continue while Iran retains control of Hormuz.' Decision not formalized (IRNA, CNBC, Al Jazeera, Irish Times, ABC News)
- ZERO-TANKER DAY APR 19: No tankers transited Hormuz Apr 19; ~230 loaded oil tankers still waiting. Commercial transit halted back to pre-Apr 17 conditions. Iran's Apr 17 'completely open' declaration fully reversed (BBC, UKMTO, NBC)
- OIL EXTENDED REBOUND (Apr 20): WTI closed Apr 20 at $88.80/bbl (+5%+ from Apr 17 $83.85 close). Brent ~$94+ tracking. Tuesday trading absorbing Trump 'Power Plants/Bridges' threat. Near $100 territory remains in play heading into Apr 22 ceasefire expiry (CNBC, Reuters, Bloomberg, Fortune)
- IRAN RECLOSES STRAIT OF HORMUZ (Apr 18): SNSC reversed Apr 17 reopening citing US "non-reciprocation" — blockade had continued despite Iran's voluntary opening. Passage will be "strictly managed" until US lifts naval blockade. Reverses single most consequential concession of ceasefire period (IRNA, Tasnim, Reuters, Al Jazeera, NYT)
- IRGC FIRES ON INDIAN-FLAGGED TANKERS (Apr 18): IRGC fast attack craft engaged VLCC Sanmar Herald (~2M bbl Iraqi crude) and Jag Arnav ~20nm NE of Oman. India MEA summoned Iran Ambassador Mohammad Fathali in "strongest possible terms" — first overt Indian diplomatic protest of war (Reuters, Hindustan Times, MEA, gCaptain, Bloomberg)
- IRAN RECLOSES STRAIT OF HORMUZ (Apr 18): SNSC reversed Apr 17 reopening citing US "non-reciprocation" — blockade had continued despite Iran's voluntary opening. Passage will be "strictly managed" until US lifts naval blockade. Reverses single most consequential concession of ceasefire period (IRNA, Tasnim, Reuters, Al Jazeera, NYT)
- IRGC FIRES ON INDIAN-FLAGGED TANKERS (Apr 18): IRGC fast attack craft engaged VLCC Sanmar Herald (~2M bbl Iraqi crude) and Jag Arnav ~20nm NE of Oman after vessels declined Iranian routing instructions; warning shots and small-arms fire, no casualties. India MEA summoned Iran Ambassador Mohammad Fathali in "strongest possible terms" — first overt Indian diplomatic protest of war (Reuters, Hindustan Times, MEA, gCaptain, Bloomberg)
- IRAN DECLARES STRAIT OF HORMUZ 'COMPLETELY OPEN' (Apr 17 — REVERSED Apr 18): FM Araghchi: 'passage for all commercial vessels through Strait of Hormuz is declared completely open for the remaining period of ceasefire.' Tied to Lebanon ceasefire. Ships must use Iranian-designated routes 'in coordination with Iranian authorities.' Iran voluntarily relinquishing central leverage chip — most consequential concession since ceasefire (NBC, NYT, BBC, Reuters, PBS, Politico, CBS)
- TRUMP: 'COMPLETELY OPEN AND READY FOR BUSINESS' — BUT BLOCKADE CONTINUES (Apr 17): Truth Social response. Minutes later: US blockade 'WILL CONTINUE UNTIL SUCH TIME AS OUR TRANSACTION WITH IRAN IS 100% COMPLETE' — 'as soon as the agreement gets signed, that's when the blockade ends' (Politico, NYT, CBS, Reuters)
- SECOND ROUND CONFIRMED MONDAY APR 21 ISLAMABAD (Apr 17-18): Pakistan-brokered. Both delegations arriving Sunday. VP Vance expected to lead US delegation. Pakistan FM Ishaq Dar: agreement 'more than 80 percent complete' but requires 'flexibility from both sides' (Dawn, Al Jazeera, The News Pakistan, Bloomberg, ARY News)
- OIL CRASHES 9-11% (Apr 17): Brent -9.07% to $90.38/bbl; WTI -11.45% to $83.85/bbl — second-largest daily drop of entire war. S&P 500 record high; Dow +869 (+1.79%); Nasdaq +2.12%. Markets pricing Apr 21 deal success (NYT, CNBC, TradingEconomics, Bloomberg, WSJ)
- FIRST CRUISE SHIP TRANSITS HORMUZ (Apr 17): Celestyal Discovery (Malta-registered) — first passenger vessel through Strait of Hormuz since war began; stranded 47 days in Dubai. BBC tracking: 'few ships moving' — commercial traffic unlikely to return to pre-war levels immediately (BBC, Reuters, Travel Weekly)
- IRAN REJECTS 'NUCLEAR DUST' FRAMING (Apr 17-18): FM spokesperson Baghaei: 'alternative facts'; 'Iran's enriched uranium is not going to be transferred anywhere.' Araghchi: 'inches away' from deal when Iran 'encountered maximalism, shifting goalposts, and blockade.' Accused US of trying 'to achieve at the negotiating table what it could not achieve through war' (WaPo, BBC, Al Jazeera, Tehran Times, Al Mayadeen)
- ISRAEL-LEBANON CEASEFIRE HOLDS DAY 5 — YELLOW LINE DEMOLITIONS ESCALATE (Apr 21): Haaretz reports paid contractors operating heavy machinery have begun demolishing homes, public buildings, and schools in the Yellow Line zone — official 'clear the area' policy. Israeli army has stopped people returning to a dozen+ villages, expanding the no-go zone. Netanyahu's announced 'ten kilometres deep' security buffer being executed as physical destruction of civilian infrastructure. Hezbollah leader Naim Qassem vows retaliation but has NOT resumed kinetic exchanges. Structural ceasefire intact through Apr 26 expiry (Haaretz, Al Jazeera, Antiwar.com, aawsat.com, Wikipedia)
- US BLOCKADE DAY 6 (Apr 17-18): Blockade continues despite Iran Hormuz reopening. CENTCOM Adm. Bradley Cooper: blockade continues 'as long as the president says it will remain in effect.' Trump maintaining pressure through Apr 21 deal closure. 13+ ships turned back Apr 13-17 (Reuters, Politico, CENTCOM, CBS)
- IRGC ABDOLLAHI: RED SEA THREAT CONTINGENT ON BLOCKADE (Apr 17): Blockade is 'illegal' ceasefire violation; if continues Iran 'would not allow any exports or imports to continue in Persian Gulf, Sea of Oman, and Red Sea under such conditions.' Positioning ahead of Apr 21 talks (Ynet, TRT World, Newsweek)
- USS BUSH STILL CIRCLING AFRICA off Namibia (avoiding Suez); USS FORD AT 300 DAYS — POST-VIETNAM RECORD FOR LONGEST CONTINUOUS US CARRIER DEPLOYMENT STILL EXTENDING. USS Boxer ARG still operating near GUAM per Apr 13 USNI tracker (Battalion Landing Team 3/5 conducting defense drills), ETA CENTCOM Apr 23-28. USS New Orleans arrived Apr 17. Apr 20 CENTCOM video confirmed USS Tripoli MH-60S Seahawks delivered VBSS team to Touska; Apr 21 Tifani interdiction in Bay of Bengal also used helicopter fast-rope. Pattern-of-two confirms doctrine. Signals strain, not surge (USNI, Stars and Stripes, The Hill, Breaking Defense, CENTCOM)
- KEY STICKING POINTS: US demands 20-year enrichment suspension; Iran offered 3-5 years. Hormuz control + compensation. Lebanon partly addressed via ceasefire
- Lebanon: ~2,161+ killed / 6,700+ wounded / 170+ children / 88+ health workers; 10-day ceasefire in effect; thousands of Lebanese returning home
- US: 15 KIA, 399 wounded (354 returned to duty); no new combat injuries during ceasefire
- HRANA: 3,597+ killed — no update since ceasefire
- Israel: 26+ killed, 7,183+ wounded; no change since ceasefire
- Mojtaba Khamenei still absent/incapacitated

### Hormuz Blockade & Regional Fallout
- IRAN DECLARES HORMUZ 'COMPLETELY OPEN' (Apr 17): FM Araghchi announced Apr 17 afternoon. Tied to Lebanon ceasefire. Ships must use Iranian-designated routes in coordination with Iranian authorities. Iran voluntarily lifting closure (NBC, NYT, BBC, Reuters, PBS)
- US BLOCKADE DAY 6 — CONTINUES (Apr 17-18): Trump maintains blockade 'until transaction 100% complete.' CENTCOM Adm. Cooper: blockade continues 'as long as the president says.' Pressure through deal closure. 13+ ships turned back since Apr 13 (Reuters, Politico, CENTCOM, CBS)
- FIRST CRUISE SHIP TRANSITS (Apr 17): Celestyal Discovery (Malta) — first passenger vessel since war began; stranded 47 days in Dubai. Commercial normalization beginning, though BBC tracking: 'few ships moving' (BBC, Reuters, Travel Weekly)
- OIL CRASHES 9-11% (Apr 17): Brent -9.07% to $90.38/bbl; WTI -11.45% to $83.85/bbl — second-largest daily drop of war. S&P 500 record high. Markets pricing Apr 21 deal success (NYT, CNBC, TradingEconomics, Bloomberg)
- IRGC ABDOLLAHI — RED SEA THREAT CONTINGENT (Apr 17): Blockade 'illegal' ceasefire violation; if continues Iran will block Persian Gulf + Sea of Oman + Red Sea. Positioning ahead of Apr 21 talks (Ynet, TRT World, Newsweek)
- UK + ALLIES REFUSE BLOCKADE: PM Starmer: 'not supporting the blockade.' Paris/London 40-nation Hormuz summit Apr 17 pursued diplomatic reopening. Iran's voluntary reopening partly preempted that track (Al Jazeera, BBC, Bloomberg)
- IMF: Middle East growth 1.1%; Iran -6.1%. Russia oil revenues doubled to $19B in March
- SAUDI CAPACITY CUT: Production -600,000 bpd; East-West pipeline -700,000 bpd (Bloomberg)
- FAO/UN FOOD CRISIS: Fertilizer 15-20% higher H1 2026; 17M Yemen at risk (FAO, UN News)

### Venezuela, Cuba, Colombia
- **MAY 18-19 UPDATE**: Alex Saab — Maduro's former 'money man,' demoted and fired by acting President Delcy Rodriguez — deported to the US May 17 and charged May 18 in Miami federal court with a single count of money laundering (decade-old food-import contract scheme; Saab was charged in 2019, then pardoned by Biden in 2023); may testify against Maduro; extends the Caracas-cooperation pattern that has 'generated enormous goodwill in Washington.' Cuban state-TV 'Con Filo' host Michel Torres Corona decried the extradition as 'shameful' ('Either Maduro is corrupt or the Rodríguez siblings are traitors') (AP via Inquirer/nvdaily/dailygazette, Breitbart, PBS NewsHour/NPR, CubaHeadlines)
- **MAY 15-16 UPDATE**: Maduro trial proceeds in NYC — judge weighing key issues, no ruling yet. Delcy Rodriguez acting president (US lifted her sanctions Apr 10); María Corina Machado weighing return; US-managed transition framing continues. CUBA grid collapse continues — Antonio Guiteras (largest plant) shut; SEN availability ~636 MW vs ~2,420 MW demand (74% unmet); Energy Min Vicente de la O: 'absolutely no fuel'; nationwide blackouts (CBS, Foreign Policy, Atlantic Council, Cuba reporting)
- **Venezuela**: Maduro PLEADED NOT GUILTY to terrorism/drug charges in Apr 13 court appearance; next hearing Mar 17, 2027. Rodriguez acting president; US lifts sanctions on Rodriguez (Apr 10); US Embassy resuming operations
- **Cuba**: CRISIS DEEPENING — blackouts affecting up to 55% of territory simultaneously. Peak deficit 1,945 MW (Apr 1). Colombia's Petro announces first shipment of solar panels. Study: $8B solar investment could cover 93.4% of demand. 100+ demonstrations in March (highest since ACLED 2018). May Day mobilization under 'The Homeland is Defended' slogan. GDP forecast -7.2%. Tourism collapsed. China helping triple solar power in one year (CiberCuba, ACLED, CubaHeadlines, WashPost)
- **Colombia**: FARC dissident clashes intensified — military killed 18th Front leader + 6 of Mordisco's security ring; EMC attacks drove 35% increase in violence in Cauca (ACLED). Petro sends solar panels to Cuba. Economic emergency Dec 2025; peace process collapsed

### Russia-Ukraine War (April 2026)
- **MAY 18-19 UPDATE**: The Black Sea Grain Initiative officially ended May 18 (Russia refusing to authorize new Ukrainian shipments absent removal of restrictions on its own agricultural/fertilizer exports). The same night, a Russian Shahed-type kamikaze drone struck a Chinese commercial vessel operating in Ukraine's territorial waters (Ukrainian Navy spokesperson Dmytro Pletenchuk). First stage of the 1,000-for-1,000 POW exchange proceeded; Putin still refuses to meet Zelensky outside Moscow; second negotiating round expected ~June 2 (Euromaidan Press, Wikipedia Black Sea Grain Initiative, Wikipedia Timeline of the Russo-Ukrainian war)
- **MAY 15-16 UPDATE**: First DIRECT Russia-Ukraine talks in 3 years held in Istanbul (Russia: Medinsky-led; Ukraine: Defense Min Umerov-led); concluded May 16 after <2 hours; NO ceasefire. Russia demanded Ukraine fully withdraw from Donetsk/Luhansk/Zaporizhzhia/Kherson (areas Russia does not fully control); Ukraine offered an immediate full/unconditional 30-day ceasefire + all-for-all POW exchange + a Zelensky-Putin meeting. Outcome: 1,000-for-1,000 POW exchange agreed; each side to present its ceasefire vision in detail; Ukraine handed over a list of forcibly deported children; Kyiv needs ~a week to review; further talks proposed June 20-30; Trump called for a direct Putin meeting (Kyiv Independent, RFE/RL, PBS, Newsweek, The Hill)
- War continues as grinding attritional conflict — Ukraine has NOT "lost"
- Russia occupies ~20% of territory; total losses ~1,313,970+
- 212 COMBAT ENGAGEMENTS IN 24 HRS (Apr 15-16): Russia lost 1,010 personnel, 50 artillery systems, 1,388 drones in single day. Russia deployed 6,672 kamikaze drones + 2,917 shelling attacks (Ukrinform, EMPR)
- DNIPRO STRUCK (Apr 14): 5 killed, 24 wounded. Apr 15 = day of mourning (Kyiv Independent, Ukrinform)
- UKRAINE-GERMANY €4B DEFENSE DEAL (Apr 14): Joint drone production (5,000 AI-enabled strike drones), Patriot missiles funded (AP, KyivPost)
- Combat resumed after Easter ceasefire expired midnight Apr 12-13. Kremlin rejected extension
- RUSSIA-UKRAINE 175 POW EXCHANGE (Apr 12): UAE mediated (Al Jazeera, PBS)
- Pokrovsk sector most active; Ukraine regained 480 sq km since late January
- Mar 10-Apr 7: Russia gained only 17 sq mi; lost 1 sq mi in last week — extremely slow advance
- Abu Dhabi talks still postponed; Iran blockade delaying Ukraine diplomacy

### US-China Trade
- **MAY 15-16 UPDATE**: Trump-Xi Beijing summit CONCLUDED Day 2 (Fri May 15) with DIVERGENT readouts. Trump claimed China would buy '200 Boeing jets' + 'fantastic trade deals'; Beijing's official statement contained ZERO mention of specific commercial agreements (no Boeing confirmation), only vague references to opening doors to US businesses. Xi emphasised Taiwan as the 'most important issue in China-US relations,' warning of 'clashes and even conflicts' if mishandled; White House readouts OMITTED Taiwan; Trump declined Taiwan questions. Both claimed progress 'stabilising ties' but 'differences remain'; agreed to a 'constructive relationship of strategic stability' (Al Jazeera, Newsweek, CNN, Euronews, CNBC). See open-questions: trump-xi-summit-iran-readout-divergence-2026
- SCOTUS struck down IEEPA tariffs Feb 20, 2026 (6-3 decision); Trump shifted to 10% global tariff under Section 122 of Trade Act
- China tariffs reduced from 57% to 47%; trade truce extended by a year
- Trump-Xi summit confirmed May 14-15 in Beijing (rescheduled from Mar 31-Apr 2 due to Iran war); reciprocal Xi visit to DC later in 2026
- Record $11.1B Taiwan arms deal announced — casting shadow over Beijing visit; Beijing: Taiwan is 'most important issue'
- China + Russia VETOED UN Security Council Hormuz resolution (Apr 7) — 11-2 vote
- US-China goods trade deficit declined 30% in 2025 to $202.1B (lowest since early 2000s)
- US bipartisan senators visited Taiwan urging $40B supplementary military budget
- KMT Chairman Eric Chu visited China Apr 7 for 'peace mission' — cross-strait diplomatic overture
- China FM said US-Israeli attacks on Iran violate international law (Apr 2)
- China launched 2 reciprocal trade investigations against US (Mar 27)
- US-Taiwan Agreement on Reciprocal Trade (ART) signed Feb 12 — $250B Taiwanese investment in US tech/chips
- USITC investigating impact of revoking China's PNTR status

### China Economy
- Real GDP growth ~2.5-3% (Rhodium estimate vs official ~5%)
- Fourth consecutive year of deflation; population declining 4 straight years
- Newborn population 7.92M (2025) — lowest since 1949
- Still world's #2 economy, largest manufacturer, dominant shipbuilder
- 2026 defense budget up 7% to ~$278B

### North Korea
- GDP grew 3.1% (2023) and 3.7% (2024) — war profiteering from Russia
- Arms sales returned $20B+; 11,000-15,000 troops deployed to Russia
- But remains one of world's poorest per capita; windfall unsustainable
- KCNA CONFIRMS CLUSTER-BOMB WARHEADS ON HWASONG-11 (Apr 8-9): Three-day testing spree included cluster-munition warhead systems on nuclear-capable Hwasong-11 missiles (Iskander-type), anti-aircraft weapons, electromagnetic weapons, carbon-fiber bombs. Missiles flew 240-700km. US/Japan: 'no immediate threat' (NPR, USNI, The Defense News)
- Strike package included NON-NUCLEAR ELECTROMAGNETIC WEAPON designed to disrupt radars, comms, fire-control, data centers, missile seekers, and command networks (The Defense News)
- Working on carbon-fiber ICBM for multi-warhead delivery (MIRV) per Seoul lawmakers
- Kim invoked Iran war to declare nuclear status 'irreversible'
- Ground-tested 'newly upgraded' solid-propellant rocket motor; max thrust 2,500 kN — 26% increase (KCNA, Mar 29; 38 North)
- DEMOLISHING VILLAGES NEAR SOHAE MISSILE/SATELLITE SITE (Bloomberg, Apr 3): Hundreds of buildings razed
- Kim visited new CEMETERY FOR SOLDIERS KILLED IN UKRAINE — burials mid-April
- Kim looking to Trump-Xi May 14-15 summit as pivotal window
- CHOE HYON DESTROYER MISSILE TESTS (Apr 14): Kim supervised launch of 2 strategic cruise missiles + 3 anti-ship missiles from 5,000-ton destroyer. Cruise missiles flew 2h12m. Kim: nuclear war deterrent 'most important priority.' 6th missile test of 2026 (NBC, Stars and Stripes, Military.com)
- Apr 8-14 testing spree preceded quiet Apr 15 (Day of the Sun) — tempo resumed Apr 19 with 7th BM test of year (see below)
- DPRK 7TH BM TEST OF YEAR FROM SINPO (Apr 19): Multiple short-range ballistic missiles fired at 6:10am KST from Sinpo area; ~140km range; Hwasong-11 family variant per KCNA. Kim hails 'accuracy.' South Korea JCS analyzing whether launch came from land-based launcher OR submarine — Sinpo is NK's major submarine shipyard. If SLBM, first in 4 years. No impact in Japan EEZ. 4th test of April (Bloomberg, Al Jazeera, NBC News, PBS News, Military.com, Euronews)
- APR 15 = DAY OF THE SUN — Kim Il Sung's 114th birthday (DPRK's biggest holiday). NK dropping 'Day of the Sun' terminology — Kim Jong Un personality cult growing over grandfather's. Tourism expo opened. No missile tests on holiday (KCNA, sedaily.com)
- China FM WANG YI VISITED PYONGYANG (Apr 9): Met NK FM Choe Son Hui — Beijing 'prepared to strengthen bilateral ties' with DPRK (AEI, Bloomberg)

### Plaza Accord
- Multilateral agreement (US, Japan, France, West Germany, UK) — not unilateral US diktat
- Japan's Finance Minister expressed willingness for yen appreciation
- Consequences were real but through indirect chain (yen appreciation → loose policy → bubble → crash)

## Status Definitions

- **confirmed**: Prediction has clearly come true
- **partially_confirmed**: Core direction correct but details wrong, or only part of the prediction materialized
- **disconfirmed**: Prediction clearly falsified by events
- **untested**: Not yet testable or insufficient time has passed
- **unfalsifiable**: Cannot be tested empirically
